thag1.1:1.0.1	Nidānagāthā
thag1.1:1.0.2	Namo tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa.
thag1.1:1.1	Sīhānaṁva nadantānaṁ,
thag1.1:1.2	dāṭhīnaṁ girigabbhare;
thag1.1:1.3	Suṇātha bhāvitattānaṁ,
thag1.1:1.4	gāthā atthūpanāyikā.
thag1.1:2.1	Yathānāmā yathāgottā,
thag1.1:2.2	yathādhammavihārino;
thag1.1:2.3	Yathādhimuttā sappaññā,
thag1.1:2.4	vihariṁsu atanditā.
thag1.1:3.1	Tattha tattha vipassitvā,
thag1.1:3.2	phusitvā accutaṁ padaṁ;
thag1.1:3.3	Katantaṁ paccavekkhantā,
thag1.1:3.4	imamatthamabhāsisuṁ.
thag1.1:4.0	Subhūtittheragāthā
thag1.1:4.1	“Channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā,
thag1.1:4.2	Vassa deva yathāsukhaṁ;
thag1.1:4.3	Cittaṁ me susamāhitaṁ vimuttaṁ,
thag1.1:4.4	Ātāpī viharāmi vassa devā”ti.
thag1.1:4.5	Itthaṁ sudaṁ āyasmā subhūtitthero gāthaṁ abhāsitthāti.

thag6.1:2.3	Tato me āsi saṁvego,
thag6.1:2.4	abbhuto lomahaṁsano.
thag6.1:3.1	Pubbe jaṭilabhūtassa,
thag6.1:3.2	yā me siddhi parittikā;
thag6.1:3.3	Tāhaṁ tadā nirākatvā,
thag6.1:3.4	pabbajiṁ jinasāsane.
thag6.1:4.1	Pubbe yaññena santuṭṭho,
thag6.1:4.2	kāmadhātupurakkhato;
thag6.1:4.3	Pacchā rāgañca dosañca,
thag6.1:4.4	mohañcāpi samūhaniṁ.
thag6.1:5.1	Pubbenivāsaṁ jānāmi,
thag6.1:5.2	dibbacakkhu visodhitaṁ;
thag6.1:5.3	Iddhimā paracittaññū,
thag6.1:5.4	dibbasotañca pāpuṇiṁ.
thag6.1:6.1	Yassa catthāya pabbajito,
thag6.1:6.2	agārasmānagāriyaṁ;
thag6.1:6.3	So me attho anuppatto,
thag6.1:6.4	sabbasaṁyojanakkhayo”ti.

thag16.8:1.1	“Gacchaṁ vadesi samaṇaṭṭhitomhi,

thag16.8:2.1	“Ṭhito ahaṁ aṅgulimāla sabbadā,

thag16.8:2.3	Tuvañca pāṇesu asaññatosi,
thag16.8:2.4	Tasmā ṭhitohaṁ tuvamaṭṭhitosi”.
thag16.8:3.1	“Cirassaṁ vata me mahito mahesī,

thag16.8:4.1	Icceva coro asimāvudhañca,
thag16.8:4.2	Sobbhe papāte narake anvakāsi;
thag16.8:4.3	Avandi coro sugatassa pāde,
thag16.8:4.4	Tattheva pabbajjamayāci buddhaṁ.
thag16.8:5.1	Buddho ca kho kāruṇiko mahesi,
thag16.8:5.2	Yo satthā lokassa sadevakassa;
thag16.8:5.3	“Tamehi bhikkhū”ti tadā avoca,
thag16.8:5.4	Eseva tassa ahu bhikkhubhāvo.
thag16.8:6.1	“Yo ca pubbe pamajjitvā,
thag16.8:6.2	pacchā so nappamajjati;

thag16.8:6.4	abbhā muttova candimā.
thag16.8:7.1	Yassa pāpaṁ kataṁ kammaṁ,
thag16.8:7.2	kusalena pidhīyati;
thag16.8:7.3	Somaṁ lokaṁ pabhāseti,
thag16.8:7.4	abbhā muttova candimā.
thag16.8:8.1	Yo have daharo bhikkhu,
thag16.8:8.2	yuñjati buddhasāsane;
thag16.8:8.3	Somaṁ lokaṁ pabhāseti,
thag16.8:8.4	abbhā muttova candimā.

thag16.8:16.3	Saraṇagamanaṁ passa,
thag16.8:16.4	bhavanetti samūhatā.

thag16.8:17.2	bahuṁ duggatigāminaṁ;

thag17.1:14.1	Lābhakāmā bhavissanti,
thag17.1:14.2	kusītā hīnavīriyā;
thag17.1:14.3	Kicchantā vanapatthāni,
thag17.1:14.4	gāmantesu vasissare.

thag17.1:16.4	sevissanti na te tadā.

thag17.1:18.4	bhikkhūnaṁ na bhavissati.

thag17.1:19.4	nāgassāsi acintiyā.
thag17.1:20.1	Chaddanto hi tadā disvā,

thag17.1:20.4	gajo atthopasaṁhitā.
thag17.1:21.1	‘Anikkasāvo kāsāvaṁ,

thag17.1:21.3	Apeto damasaccena,
thag17.1:21.4	na so kāsāvamarahati.

thag17.1:22.2	sīlesu susamāhito;
thag17.1:22.3	Upeto damasaccena,
thag17.1:22.4	sa ve kāsāvamarahati.

thag17.1:23.2	pākaṭo kāmakāriyo;
thag17.1:23.3	Vibbhantacitto nissukko,
thag17.1:23.4	na so kāsāvamarahati.

thag17.1:24.2	vītarāgo samāhito;

thag17.1:24.4	sa ve kāsāvamarahati.

thag17.1:25.3	Odātakaṁ arahati,
thag17.1:25.4	kāsāvaṁ kiṁ karissati.

thag17.1:27.4	pākaṭā kāmakāriyā.

thag17.1:28.4	khaḷuṅko viya sārathiṁ.

thag21.1:1.1	“Nikkhantaṁ vata maṁ santaṁ,

thag21.1:6.1	Aratiñca ratiñca pahāya,

thag21.1:8.4	Yo hettha na limpati muni tamāhu.

thag21.1:50.4	anukampāya pāṇinaṁ.
thag21.1:51.1	Dukkhaṁ dukkhasamuppādaṁ,
thag21.1:51.2	Dukkhassa ca atikkamaṁ;
thag21.1:51.3	Ariyaṁ caṭṭhaṅgikaṁ maggaṁ,
thag21.1:51.4	Dukkhūpasamagāminaṁ.
thag21.1:52.1	Evamete tathā vuttā,
thag21.1:52.2	diṭṭhā me te yathā tathā;
thag21.1:52.3	Sadattho me anuppatto,
thag21.1:52.4	kataṁ buddhassa sāsanaṁ.
thag21.1:53.1	Svāgataṁ vata me āsi,
thag21.1:53.2	mama buddhassa santike;
thag21.1:53.3	Suvibhattesu dhammesu,
thag21.1:53.4	yaṁ seṭṭhaṁ tadupāgamiṁ.
thag21.1:54.1	Abhiññāpāramippatto,
thag21.1:54.2	sotadhātu visodhitā;
thag21.1:54.3	Tevijjo iddhipattomhi,
thag21.1:54.4	cetopariyakovido”.
thag21.1:55.1	“Pucchāmi satthāramanomapaññaṁ,
thag21.1:55.2	Diṭṭheva dhamme yo vicikicchānaṁ chettā;
thag21.1:55.3	Aggāḷave kālamakāsi bhikkhu,
thag21.1:55.4	Ñāto yasassī abhinibbutatto.
thag21.1:56.1	Nigrodhakappo iti tassa nāmaṁ,
thag21.1:56.2	Tayā kataṁ bhagavā brāhmaṇassa;
thag21.1:56.3	So taṁ namassaṁ acari mutyapekho,
thag21.1:56.4	Āraddhavīriyo daḷhadhammadassī.
thag21.1:57.1	Taṁ sāvakaṁ sakka mayampi sabbe,
thag21.1:57.2	Aññātumicchāma samantacakkhu;
thag21.1:57.3	Samavaṭṭhitā no savanāya sotā,
thag21.1:57.4	Tuvaṁ no satthā tvamanuttarosi.
thag21.1:58.1	Chinda no vicikicchaṁ brūhi metaṁ,
thag21.1:58.2	Parinibbutaṁ vedaya bhūripañña;
thag21.1:58.3	Majjheva no bhāsa samantacakkhu,
thag21.1:58.4	Sakkova devāna sahassanetto.
thag21.1:59.1	Ye keci ganthā idha mohamaggā,
thag21.1:59.2	Aññāṇapakkhā vicikicchaṭhānā;
thag21.1:59.3	Tathāgataṁ patvā na te bhavanti,
thag21.1:59.4	Cakkhuñhi etaṁ paramaṁ narānaṁ.
thag21.1:60.1	No ce hi jātu puriso kilese,
thag21.1:60.2	Vāto yathā abbhaghanaṁ vihāne;
thag21.1:60.3	Tamovassa nivuto sabbaloko,
thag21.1:60.4	Jotimantopi na pabhāseyyuṁ.
thag21.1:61.1	Dhīrā ca pajjotakarā bhavanti,
thag21.1:61.2	Taṁ taṁ ahaṁ vīra tatheva maññe;
thag21.1:61.3	Vipassinaṁ jānamupāgamimha,
thag21.1:61.4	Parisāsu no āvikarohi kappaṁ.
thag21.1:62.1	Khippaṁ giraṁ eraya vaggu vagguṁ,
thag21.1:62.2	Haṁsova paggayha saṇikaṁ nikūja;
thag21.1:62.3	Bindussarena suvikappitena,
thag21.1:62.4	Sabbeva te ujjugatā suṇoma.
thag21.1:63.1	Pahīnajātimaraṇaṁ asesaṁ,
thag21.1:63.2	Niggayha dhonaṁ vadessāmi dhammaṁ;
thag21.1:63.3	Na kāmakāro hi puthujjanānaṁ,
thag21.1:63.4	Saṅkheyyakāro ca tathāgatānaṁ.
thag21.1:64.1	Sampannaveyyākaraṇaṁ tavedaṁ,
thag21.1:64.2	Samujjupaññassa samuggahītaṁ;
thag21.1:64.3	Ayamañjali pacchimo suppaṇāmito,
thag21.1:64.4	Mā mohayī jānamanomapañña.
thag21.1:65.1	Paroparaṁ ariyadhammaṁ viditvā,
thag21.1:65.2	Mā mohayī jānamanomavīriya;
thag21.1:65.3	Vāriṁ yathā ghammani ghammatatto,
thag21.1:65.4	Vācābhikaṅkhāmi sutaṁ pavassa.
thag21.1:66.1	Yadatthikaṁ brahmacariyaṁ acarī,
thag21.1:66.2	Kappāyano kaccissataṁ amoghaṁ;
thag21.1:66.3	Nibbāyi so ādu saupādiseso,
thag21.1:66.4	Yathā vimutto ahu taṁ suṇoma”.
thag21.1:67.1	“Acchecchi taṇhaṁ idha nāmarūpe,
thag21.1:67.2	(iti bhagavā)
thag21.1:67.3	Kaṇhassa sotaṁ dīgharattānusayitaṁ;
thag21.1:67.4	Atāri jātiṁ maraṇaṁ asesaṁ,
thag21.1:67.5	Iccabravi bhagavā pañcaseṭṭho”.
thag21.1:68.1	“Esa sutvā pasīdāmi,
thag21.1:68.2	vaco te isisattama;
thag21.1:68.3	Amoghaṁ kira me puṭṭhaṁ,
thag21.1:68.4	na maṁ vañcesi brāhmaṇo.
thag21.1:69.1	Yathā vādī tathā kārī,
thag21.1:69.2	ahu buddhassa sāvako;
thag21.1:69.3	Acchecchi maccuno jālaṁ,
thag21.1:69.4	tataṁ māyāvino daḷhaṁ.
thag21.1:70.1	Addasa bhagavā ādiṁ,
thag21.1:70.2	upādānassa kappiyo;
thag21.1:70.3	Accagā vata kappāno,
thag21.1:70.4	maccudheyyaṁ suduttaraṁ.
thag21.1:71.1	Taṁ devadevaṁ vandāmi,
thag21.1:71.2	puttaṁ te dvipaduttama;
thag21.1:71.3	Anujātaṁ mahāvīraṁ,
thag21.1:71.4	nāgaṁ nāgassa orasan”ti.
thag21.1:71.5	Itthaṁ sudaṁ āyasmā vaṅgīso thero gāthāyo abhāsitthāti.
thag21.1:71.6	Mahānipāto niṭṭhito.
thag21.1:72.0	Tatruddānaṁ
thag21.1:72.1	Sattatimhi nipātamhi,
thag21.1:72.2	vaṅgīso paṭibhāṇavā;
thag21.1:72.3	Ekova thero natthañño,
thag21.1:72.4	gāthāyo ekasattatīti.
thag21.1:72.5	Niṭṭhitā theragāthāyo.
thag21.1:73.0	Tatruddānaṁ
thag21.1:73.1	Sahassaṁ honti tā gāthā,
thag21.1:73.2	tīṇi saṭṭhisatāni ca;
thag21.1:73.3	Therā ca dve satā saṭṭhi,
thag21.1:73.4	cattāro ca pakāsitā.
thag21.1:74.1	Sīhanādaṁ naditvāna,
thag21.1:74.2	buddhaputtā anāsavā;
thag21.1:74.3	Khemantaṁ pāpuṇitvāna,
thag21.1:74.4	aggikhandhāva nibbutāti.
thag21.1:74.5	Theragāthāpāḷi niṭṭhitā.
